My friends have been busy too, so it’s hard to tell if I’m avoiding stuff or if schedules just don’t line up. I guess I’m not sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or depressed most of the day, nearly every day, for at least the past two weeks?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. After my energy dropped like that, I started feeling kind of empty most days, and hopeless, like I’d already messed up my whole future in healthcare before it even really started. And then I get stuck on that thought, like even at work when I’m just supposed to be doing basic stuff.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way? Has it been going on for more than two weeks, or did it start more recently?\n\nPatient: It’s definitely been more than two weeks. I mean, I keep trying to think of when it really started, because days kind of blur together, especially with work and just getting through stuff. But the depressed, guilty, exhausted feeling has been going on for… maybe about nine months now, since after I kind of crashed from that high-energy stretch before. It didn’t just start recently.\n\nDoctor: Have you had this depressed mood or low energy for most of the time over the past two years?\n\nPatient: Not over the whole two years, no… I don’t think so. Like, I’ve had bad weeks before, I guess, but I don’t know if I’d call them depression or if they lasted long enough like that. School was stressful sometimes, and I’d get into these moods where I just didn’t want to deal with people, but then something would happen and I’d be distracted, or I’d have work, or… I don’t know.\n\nSo before this current thing, I can’t really point to a clear couple-week period and say, yeah, that was the same. This one feels more obvious because it just kept going.\n\nDoctor: Before this current nine-month period, was there ever a time when you felt depressed or had low energy for at least two weeks straight?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know.
